* duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g2&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4]&gt;, am1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5]&gt;, ap1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;, ut=duo_lmars_ustar_a&lt;a=duo_lmars_a&lt;piE=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], piW=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4]&gt;, piE=duo_recon_vm_g2&lt;a0=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], am1=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], ap1=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4]&gt;, piW=duo_recon_vm_g2&lt;a0=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], am1=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], ap1=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, uE=sqrt(1 - duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt; * duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g2&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4]&gt;, am1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5]&gt;, ap1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;) * (1 / duo_arc_length&lt;ax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], ay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], az=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], bx=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], by=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], bz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], radius=R_sphere&gt;)) where {gi in x, gj in y, gt in tile}</div>

Per-cell operator for momentum_v_duo_g2 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule momentum_v_duo_g2 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Vector-invariant momentum tendency for the covariant v-wind, duo gnomonic cubed-sphere SWE core, Ng=2, WITHOUT the pressure gradient (added by a separate pgrad_*_v rule so both L97 and direct forms are drop-in). Chen (2021) C36/C37 as the Zenodo sw_full: dv/dt = -(u^contra)*sin(alpha)*(f+zeta) + (ke(i)-ke(i+1))/Dy. The absolute vorticity f+zeta is the circulation [dx(vp*Lx)-dy(up*Ly)]/A + f with up,vp the UPWINDED parallel velocities reused from the LMARS solve (Eq 9/C38); the interface kinetic energy is 1/2(u*^2 + up^2) in the local orthogonal frame (Eq 10); (f+zeta) multiplies the contravariant cross-velocity times sin(alpha)=sqrt(g) (C36/C37); Dy is the great-circle arc between the two y-face midpoints. Operates on halo-extended dp,ua,va; f is the problem-supplied Coriolis parameter 2 Omega sin(theta) on [tile,x,y]. Method-of-lines spatial RHS.

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

### `momentum_v_duo_g3_interior`

Per-cell operator for momentum_v_duo_g3 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule momentum_v_duo_g3 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Vector-invariant momentum tendency for the covariant v-wind, duo gnomonic cubed-sphere SWE core, Ng=3, WITHOUT the pressure gradient (added by a separate pgrad_*_v rule so both L97 and direct forms are drop-in). Chen (2021) C36/C37 as the Zenodo sw_full: dv/dt = -(u^contra)*sin(alpha)*(f+zeta) + (ke(i)-ke(i+1))/Dy. The absolute vorticity f+zeta is the circulation [dx(vp*Lx)-dy(up*Ly)]/A + f with up,vp the UPWINDED parallel velocities reused from the LMARS solve (Eq 9/C38); the interface kinetic energy is 1/2(u*^2 + up^2) in the local orthogonal frame (Eq 10); (f+zeta) multiplies the contravariant cross-velocity times sin(alpha)=sqrt(g) (C36/C37); Dy is the great-circle arc between the two y-face midpoints. Operates on halo-extended dp,ua,va; f is the problem-supplied Coriolis parameter 2 Omega sin(theta) on [tile,x,y]. Method-of-lines spatial RHS.

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

* duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;&gt; + duo_lmars_pibar&lt;piE=duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=phis[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3]&gt;, piW=duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=phis[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;&gt; - (duo_lmars_pibar&lt;piE=duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, piW=duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=dp[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3]&gt;&gt; + duo_lmars_pivis&lt;piE=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], piW=dp[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], uE=sqrt(1 - duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt; * duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;, uW=sqrt(1 - duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt; * duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;&gt; + duo_lmars_pibar&lt;piE=duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=phis[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, piW=duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=phis[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;)) * (1 / duo_arc_length&lt;ax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], ay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], az=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], bx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], by=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], bz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], radius=R_sphere&gt;)) where {gi in x, gj in y, gt in tile}</div>

Per-cell operator for pgrad_direct_u_duo_g1 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_direct_u_duo_g1 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant u-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=1. Direct LMARS-Pi* pressure gradient -dx(Pibar + Pivis + Phi_s)/Dx, mathematically equivalent to the L97 four-corner form for SW (paper) but written as a plain interface-pressure difference. Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dx the arc between x-face midpoints. Add to momentum_u_duo_g1 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

+ duo_lmars_pibar&lt;piE=duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=phis[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], am1=phis[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3], am2=phis[gt, gi, gj + 3], ap1=phis[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], ap2=phis[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3]&gt;, piW=duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=phis[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], am1=phis[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], am2=phis[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], ap1=phis[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], ap2=phis[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3]&gt;&gt; - (duo_lmars_pibar&lt;piE=duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], am1=dp[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], am2=dp[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3], ap1=dp[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], ap2=dp[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3]&gt;, piW=duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=dp[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], am1=dp[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], am2=dp[gt, gi + 6, gj + 3], ap1=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], ap2=dp[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3]&gt;&gt; + duo_lmars_pivis&lt;piE=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], piW=dp[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], uE=sqrt(1 - duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt; * duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, am1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3]&gt;, am2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3]&gt;, ap1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3]&gt;, ap2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;, uW=sqrt(1 - duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt; * duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3]&gt;, am1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3]&gt;, am2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 6, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 6, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 6, gj + 3]&gt;, ap1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, ap2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;&gt; + duo_lmars_pibar&lt;piE=duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=phis[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], am1=phis[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], am2=phis[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3], ap1=phis[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], ap2=phis[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3]&gt;, piW=duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=phis[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], am1=phis[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], am2=phis[gt, gi + 6, gj + 3], ap1=phis[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], ap2=phis[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;)) * (1 / duo_arc_length&lt;ax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], ay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], az=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], bx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], by=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], bz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], radius=R_sphere&gt;)) where {gi in x, gj in y, gt in tile}</div>

Per-cell operator for pgrad_direct_u_duo_g3 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_direct_u_duo_g3 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant u-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=3. Direct LMARS-Pi* pressure gradient -dx(Pibar + Pivis + Phi_s)/Dx, mathematically equivalent to the L97 four-corner form for SW (paper) but written as a plain interface-pressure difference. Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dx the arc between x-face midpoints. Add to momentum_u_duo_g3 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

Per-cell operator for pgrad_direct_v_duo_g2 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_direct_v_duo_g2 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant v-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=2. Direct LMARS-Pi* pressure gradient -dx(Pibar + Pivis + Phi_s)/Dx, mathematically equivalent to the L97 four-corner form for SW (paper) but written as a plain interface-pressure difference. Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dy the arc between y-face midpoints. Add to momentum_v_duo_g2 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

Per-cell operator for pgrad_direct_v_duo_g3 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_direct_v_duo_g3 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant v-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=3. Direct LMARS-Pi* pressure gradient -dx(Pibar + Pivis + Phi_s)/Dx, mathematically equivalent to the L97 four-corner form for SW (paper) but written as a plain interface-pressure difference. Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dy the arc between y-face midpoints. Add to momentum_v_duo_g3 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

Per-cell operator for pgrad_l97_u_duo_g1 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_l97_u_duo_g1 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant u-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=1. L97 four-corner geometric pressure-gradient (Lin 1997) plus the LMARS viscous term, the Zenodo sw_full DEFAULT (Eqs 20-26; akap=1, ptop=0 for SW so pk=pe, gz=Phi_s+Pi and the four-corner du_hydro reduces to the geometric -dx(avg(Phi_s+Pi))/Dx, with the viscous du_pp = rdx/dp*(Pibar_i*Pivis_i - Pibar_i+1*Pivis_i+1)). Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dx the arc between x-face midpoints. Add to momentum_u_duo_g1 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

Per-cell operator for pgrad_l97_u_duo_g2 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_l97_u_duo_g2 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant u-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=2. L97 four-corner geometric pressure-gradient (Lin 1997) plus the LMARS viscous term, the Zenodo sw_full DEFAULT (Eqs 20-26; akap=1, ptop=0 for SW so pk=pe, gz=Phi_s+Pi and the four-corner du_hydro reduces to the geometric -dx(avg(Phi_s+Pi))/Dx, with the viscous du_pp = rdx/dp*(Pibar_i*Pivis_i - Pibar_i+1*Pivis_i+1)). Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dx the arc between x-face midpoints. Add to momentum_u_duo_g2 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

* duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, am1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3]&gt;, am2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 1, gj + 3]&gt;, ap1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3]&gt;, ap2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;, uW=sqrt(1 - duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt; * duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3]&gt;, am1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 5, gj + 3]&gt;, am2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 6, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 6, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 6, gj + 3]&gt;, ap1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, ap2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], u=ua[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3], v=va[gt, gi + 2, gj + 3]&gt;&gt;&gt;) / dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3])) where {gi in x, gj in y, gt in tile}</div>

Per-cell operator for pgrad_l97_u_duo_g3 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_l97_u_duo_g3 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant u-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=3. L97 four-corner geometric pressure-gradient (Lin 1997) plus the LMARS viscous term, the Zenodo sw_full DEFAULT (Eqs 20-26; akap=1, ptop=0 for SW so pk=pe, gz=Phi_s+Pi and the four-corner du_hydro reduces to the geometric -dx(avg(Phi_s+Pi))/Dx, with the viscous du_pp = rdx/dp*(Pibar_i*Pivis_i - Pibar_i+1*Pivis_i+1)). Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dx the arc between x-face midpoints. Add to momentum_u_duo_g3 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

* duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g1&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2]&gt;&gt;, uW=sqrt(1 - duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3]&gt; Per-cell operator for pgrad_l97_v_duo_g1 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_l97_v_duo_g1 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant v-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=1. L97 four-corner geometric pressure-gradient (Lin 1997) plus the LMARS viscous term, the Zenodo sw_full DEFAULT (Eqs 20-26; akap=1, ptop=0 for SW so pk=pe, gz=Phi_s+Pi and the four-corner du_hydro reduces to the geometric -dx(avg(Phi_s+Pi))/Dx, with the viscous du_pp = rdx/dp*(Pibar_i*Pivis_i - Pibar_i+1*Pivis_i+1)). Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dy the arc between y-face midpoints. Add to momentum_v_duo_g1 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

Per-cell operator for pgrad_l97_v_duo_g2 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_l97_v_duo_g2 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant v-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=2. L97 four-corner geometric pressure-gradient (Lin 1997) plus the LMARS viscous term, the Zenodo sw_full DEFAULT (Eqs 20-26; akap=1, ptop=0 for SW so pk=pe, gz=Phi_s+Pi and the four-corner du_hydro reduces to the geometric -dx(avg(Phi_s+Pi))/Dx, with the viscous du_pp = rdx/dp*(Pibar_i*Pivis_i - Pibar_i+1*Pivis_i+1)). Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dy the arc between y-face midpoints. Add to momentum_v_duo_g2 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

* duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, am1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4]&gt;, am2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5]&gt;, ap1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2]&gt;, ap2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 1], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 1], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 1]&gt;&gt;&gt; - duo_lmars_pibar&lt;piE=duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], am1=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], am2=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 1], ap1=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], ap2=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5]&gt;, piW=duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], am1=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], am2=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 6], ap1=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], ap2=dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2]&gt;&gt; * duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, am1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2]&gt;, am2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 1], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 1], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 1]&gt;, ap1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4]&gt;, ap2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5]&gt;&gt;, uW=sqrt(1 - duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt; * duo_tangent_cos&lt;kax=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kay=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kaz=node[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3] + node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3], kbx=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], kby=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], kbz=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], kcx=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 1], kcy=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 2], kcz=node[gt, gi + 4, gj + 4, 3]&gt;) * duo_recon_vm_g3&lt;a0=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4]&gt;, am1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 5]&gt;, am2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 6], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 6], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 6]&gt;, ap1=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3]&gt;, ap2=duo_contravariant&lt;cosA=cosA[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], u=va[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2], v=ua[gt, gi + 3, gj + 2]&gt;&gt;&gt;) / dp[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3])) where {gi in x, gj in y, gt in tile}</div>

Per-cell operator for pgrad_l97_v_duo_g3 as a match-less named stencil (body = aggregate over the tile/x/y loop indices gt/gi/gj, indexing prognostics relative to those loop vars with the +3 donor-cube halo offset). The rule pgrad_l97_v_duo_g3 is a thin makearray wrapper that applies this once. Original: Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant v-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=3. L97 four-corner geometric pressure-gradient (Lin 1997) plus the LMARS viscous term, the Zenodo sw_full DEFAULT (Eqs 20-26; akap=1, ptop=0 for SW so pk=pe, gz=Phi_s+Pi and the four-corner du_hydro reduces to the geometric -dx(avg(Phi_s+Pi))/Dx, with the viscous du_pp = rdx/dp*(Pibar_i*Pivis_i - Pibar_i+1*Pivis_i+1)). Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dy the arc between y-face midpoints. Add to momentum_v_duo_g3 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

## Rules

Complete auto-applied rewrite rules on spatial `D`: imported stencil + boundary conditions in one `makearray` (esm-spec §9.6.8). Import a rule and every matching derivative in your model lowers through it.

### `duo_corner_diag`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:corner_fill</code> <code>order:4</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x_cell_ext,y_cell_ext</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/duo_corner_diag.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/duo_corner_diag.esm)

**Discretization** — one `makearray` tiling the output axes; boundary conditions live in the face regions of this same rule (esm-spec §9.6.8; later regions overwrite earlier, §4.3.2). Imported stencils appear as template applications `name<param=value, ...>`, defined in the Stencils section of this page:

<div class="math-block">makearray([1:6, 1:N + 6, 1:N + 6] = sum[gt, gi, gj] (q[gt, gi, gj]) where {gi in x_cell_ext, gj in y_cell_ext, gt in tile}, [1:6, N + 4:N + 6, N + 4:N + 6] = sum[gt, gi, gj] (duo_corner_sel_1&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 1], p10=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 1], p11=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 2], p12=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 3], p13=q[gt, N, gj], p14=q[gt, N + 1, gj], 2], p28=center[gt, gi, N + 3, 3], p29=q[gt, gi, N], p3=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 3], p30=q[gt, gi, N + 1], p31=q[gt, gi, N + 2], p32=q[gt, gi, N + 3], p4=center[gt, N, gj, 1], p5=center[gt, N, gj, 2], p6=center[gt, N, gj, 3], p7=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 1], p8=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 2], p9=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 3], q=q&gt;) where {gi in N + 4:N + 6, gj in N + 4:N + 6, gt in tile}, [1:6, 1:3, 1:3] = sum[gt, gi, gj] (duo_corner_sel_1&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, 5, gj, 1], p10=center[gt, 7, gj, 1], p11=center[gt, 7, gj, 2], p12=center[gt, 7, gj, 3], p13=q[gt, 4, gj], p14=q[gt, 5, gj], p15=q[gt, gj, 3], p30=q[gt, gi, 5], p31=q[gt, gi, 6], p32=q[gt, gi, 7], p4=center[gt, 4, gj, 1], p5=center[gt, 4, gj, 2], p6=center[gt, 4, gj, 3], p7=center[gt, 6, gj, 1], p8=center[gt, 6, gj, 2], p9=center[gt, (duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, 5, gj, 1], p10=center[gt, 7, gj, 1], p11=center[gt, 7, gj, 2], p12=center[gt, 7, gj, 3], p13=q[gt, 4, gj], p2=center[gt, 5, gj, 2], p26=q[gt, 5, gj], p3=center[gt, 5, gj, 3], p39=q[gt, 6, gj], p4=center[gt, 4, gj, 1], p5=center[gt, 4, gj, 2], p52=q[gt, 7, gj], p6=center[gt, 4, gj, 3], p7=center[gt, 6, gj, 1], p8=center[gt, 6, gj, 2], p9=center[gt, (ifelse((gi - (N + 3) + gj - 4) * (gi - (N + 3) + gj - 4) &lt; 0.5, 0.5 * (duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 1], p10=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 1], p11=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 2], p12=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 3], p13=q[gt, N, gj], p2=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 2], p26=q[gt, N + 1, gj], p3=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 3], p39=q[gt, N + 2, gj], p4=center[gt, N, gj, 1], p5=center[gt, N, gj, 2], p52=q[gt, N + 3, gj], p6=center[gt, N, gj, 3], p7=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 1], p8=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 2], p9=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 3]&gt; + duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi, 5, 1], p10=center[gt, gi, 7, 1], p11=center[gt, gi, 7, 2], p12=center[gt, gi, 7, 3], p13=q[gt, gi, 4], p2=center[gt, gi, 5, 2], p26=q[gt, gi, 5], p3=center[gt, gi, 5, 3], p39=q[gt, gi, 6], p4=center[gt, gi, 4, 1], p5=center[gt, gi, 4, 2], p52=q[gt, gi, 7], p6=center[gt, gi, 4, 3], p7=center[gt, gi, 6, 1], p8=center[gt, gi, 6, 2], p9=center[gt, gi, 6, 3]&gt;), q[gt, gi, gj])) where {gi in N + 4:N + 6, gj in 1:3, gt in tile})</div>

PASS 2 of the duo corner fill: overwrite the 3 DIAGONAL points of each corner by 0.5*(X-interp + Y-interp) over the off-diagonal-filled field (Zerroukat eq 10). Bind q to the duo_corner_offdiag output. Duo-grid CUBE-CORNER halo fill (Zerroukat & Allen 2021 QJRMS 10.1002/qj.4230; Mouallem 2023 fv_duogrid.F90 fill_corner_region_2d) for a cell-centred A-grid field, to cubic (order-4) accuracy, needed by TRULY 2-D (non-split) stencils -- the direction-split LMARS reconstructions never read the corner blocks, so duo_extend leaves them unfilled. Projection-agnostic: barycentric-Lagrange weights from GREAT-CIRCLE distances between cell centres (via the shared duo_arc_length atom), so equidistance/equiangular/equi-edge alike. Each corner's 3x3 halo block is one aggregate with an ifelse selecting the interpolation direction on the two off-diagonal triangles. TWO passes because the diagonal reads the just-filled off-diagonal. Bind center to duo_cell_center; R_sphere is a consumer free name. Verified bit-identical to a numeric reimplementation of the Fortran over all 4 corners; converges at ~4th order.

**References**

- Zerroukat, M., & Allen, T. (2022). On the corners of the cubed-sphere grid. QJRMS, 148(743), 778-783.
- Mouallem, J., Harris, L., & Chen, X. (2023). Implementation of the novel Duo-Grid in GFDL's FV3. JAMES, 15, e2023MS003712.

**Conformance fixtures**

- ast: [`duo_corner_diag`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/ast/duo_corner_diag) — status `active`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:corner_fill</code> <code>order:4</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x_cell_ext,y_cell_ext</code></p>

<div class="math-block">makearray([1:6, 1:N + 6, 1:N + 6] = sum[gt, gi, gj] (q[gt, gi, gj]) where {gi in x_cell_ext, gj in y_cell_ext, gt in tile}, [1:6, N + 4:N + 6, N + 4:N + 6] = sum[gt, gi, gj] (ifelse(gi &lt; gj, duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 1], p10=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 1], p11=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 2], p12=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 3], p13=q[gt, N, gj], p2=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 2], p26=q[gt, N + 1, gj], p3=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 3], p39=q[gt, N + 2, gj], p4=center[gt, N, gj, 1], p5=center[gt, N, gj, 2], p52=q[gt, N + 3, gj], p6=center[gt, N, gj, 3], p7=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 1], p8=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 2], p9=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 3]&gt;, ifelse(gi &gt; gj, duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, 5, gj, 1], p10=center[gt, 7, gj, 1], p11=center[gt, 7, gj, 2], p12=center[gt, 7, gj, 3], p13=q[gt, 4, gj], p2=center[gt, 5, gj, 2], p26=q[gt, 5, gj], p3=center[gt, 5, gj, 3], p39=q[gt, 6, gj], p4=center[gt, 4, gj, 1], p5=center[gt, 4, gj, 2], p52=q[gt, 7, gj], p6=center[gt, 4, gj, 3], p7=center[gt, 6, gj, 1], p8=center[gt, 6, gj, 2], p9=center[gt, 6, gj, 3]&gt;, ifelse(gi &lt; gj, duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi, 5, 1], p10=center[gt, gi, 7, 1], p11=center[gt, gi, 7, 2], p12=center[gt, gi, 7, 3], p13=q[gt, gi, 4], p2=center[gt, gi, 5, 2], p26=q[gt, gi, 5], p3=center[gt, gi, 5, 3], p39=q[gt, gi, 6], p4=center[gt, gi, 4, 1], p5=center[gt, gi, 4, 2], p52=q[gt, gi, 7], p6=center[gt, gi, 4, 3], p7=center[gt, gi, 6, 1], p8=center[gt, gi, 6, 2], p9=center[gt, gi, 6, 3]&gt;, q[gt, gi, gj]))) where {gi in 1:3, gj in 1:3, gt in tile}, [1:6, 1:3, N + 4:N + 6] = sum[gt, gi, gj] (ifelse(gi + (gj - (N + 3)) &gt; 4, duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, 5, gj, 1], p10=center[gt, 7, gj, 1], p11=center[gt, 7, gj, 2], p12=center[gt, 7, gj, 3], p13=q[gt, 4, gj], p2=center[gt, 5, gj, 2], p26=q[gt, 5, gj], p3=center[gt, 5, gj, 3], p39=q[gt, 6, gj], p4=center[gt, 4, gj, 1], p5=center[gt, 4, gj, 2], p52=q[gt, 7, gj], p6=center[gt, 4, gj, 3], p7=center[gt, 6, gj, 1], p8=center[gt, 6, gj, 2], p9=center[gt, 6, gj, 3]&gt;, ifelse(gi + (gj - (N + 3)) &lt; 4, duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi, N + 1, 1], p10=center[gt, gi, N + 3, 1], p11=center[gt, gi, N + 3, 2], p12=center[gt, gi, N + 3, 3], p13=q[gt, gi, N], p2=center[gt, gi, N + 1, 2], p26=q[gt, gi, N + 1], p3=center[gt, gi, N + 1, 3], p39=q[gt, gi, N + 2], p4=center[gt, gi, N, 1], p5=center[gt, gi, N, 2], p52=q[gt, gi, N + 3], p6=center[gt, gi, N, 3], p7=center[gt, gi, N + 2, 1], p8=center[gt, gi, N + 2, 2], p9=center[gt, gi, N + 2, 3]&gt;, q[gt, gi, gj]))) where {gi in 1:3, gj in N + 4:N + 6, gt in tile}, [1:6, N + 4:N + 6, 1:3] = sum[gt, gi, gj] (ifelse(gi - (N + 3) + gj &lt; 4, duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 1], p10=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 1], p11=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 2], p12=center[gt, N + 3, gj, 3], p13=q[gt, N, gj], p2=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 2], p26=q[gt, N + 1, gj], p3=center[gt, N + 1, gj, 3], p39=q[gt, N + 2, gj], p4=center[gt, N, gj, 1], p5=center[gt, N, gj, 2], p52=q[gt, N + 3, gj], p6=center[gt, N, gj, 3], p7=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 1], p8=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 2], p9=center[gt, N + 2, gj, 3]&gt;, ifelse(gi - (N + 3) + gj &gt; 4, duo_corner_assy_3&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi, 5, 1], p10=center[gt, gi, 7, 1], p11=center[gt, gi, 7, 2], p12=center[gt, gi, 7, 3], p13=q[gt, gi, 4], p2=center[gt, gi, 5, 2], p26=q[gt, gi, 5], p3=center[gt, gi, 5, 3], p39=q[gt, gi, 6], p4=center[gt, gi, 4, 1], p5=center[gt, gi, 4, 2], p52=q[gt, gi, 7], p6=center[gt, gi, 4, 3], p7=center[gt, gi, 6, 1], p8=center[gt, gi, 6, 2], p9=center[gt, gi, 6, 3]&gt;, q[gt, gi, gj]))) where {gi in N + 4:N + 6, gj in 1:3, gt in tile})</div>

PASS 1 of the duo corner fill: overwrite the 6 OFF-DIAGONAL points of each of the 4 corners by 1-D Lagrange extension of the adjacent edge lines (Zerroukat eqs 8-9). Bind q to the edge-filled halo (duo_extend output). Duo-grid CUBE-CORNER halo fill (Zerroukat & Allen 2021 QJRMS 10.1002/qj.4230; Mouallem 2023 fv_duogrid.F90 fill_corner_region_2d) for a cell-centred A-grid field, to cubic (order-4) accuracy, needed by TRULY 2-D (non-split) stencils -- the direction-split LMARS reconstructions never read the corner blocks, so duo_extend leaves them unfilled. Projection-agnostic: barycentric-Lagrange weights from GREAT-CIRCLE distances between cell centres (via the shared duo_arc_length atom), so equidistance/equiangular/equi-edge alike. Each corner's 3x3 halo block is one aggregate with an ifelse selecting the interpolation direction on the two off-diagonal triangles. TWO passes because the diagonal reads the just-filled off-diagonal. Bind center to duo_cell_center; R_sphere is a consumer free name. Verified bit-identical to a numeric reimplementation of the Fortran over all 4 corners; converges at ~4th order.

**References**

- Zerroukat, M., & Allen, T. (2022). On the corners of the cubed-sphere grid. QJRMS, 148(743), 778-783.
- Mouallem, J., Harris, L., & Chen, X. (2023). Implementation of the novel Duo-Grid in GFDL's FV3. JAMES, 15, e2023MS003712.

**Conformance fixtures**

- ast: [`duo_corner_offdiag`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/ast/duo_corner_offdiag) — status `active`

### `duo_extend`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:halo_extend</code> <code>order:2</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x_cell_ext,y_cell_ext</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/duo_extend.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/duo_extend.esm)

**Discretization** — one `makearray` tiling the output axes; boundary conditions live in the face regions of this same rule (esm-spec §9.6.8; later regions overwrite earlier, §4.3.2). Imported stencils appear as template applications `name<param=value, ...>`, defined in the Stencils section of this page:

<div class="math-block">makearray([1:6, 1:N + 6, 1:N + 6] = sum[gt, gi, gj] (q[gt, max(1, min(N, (gi - 3))), max(1, min(N, (gj - 3)))]) where {gi in x_cell_ext, gj in y_cell_ext, gt in tile}, [1:1, 1:3, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_w&lt;X=(-center[1, gi, gj, 3]) / (-center[1, gi, gj, 2]), Y=center[1, gi, gj, 1] / (-center[1, gi, gj, 2]), center=center, dt=5, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [1:1, N + 4:N + 6, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_e&lt;X=(-center[1, gi, gj, 1]) / center[1, gi, gj, 2], Y=center[1, gi, gj, 3] / center[1, gi, gj, 2], center=center, dt=2, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [1:1, 4:N + 3, 1:3] = duo_extend_strip_s&lt;X=center[1, gi, gj, 2] / (-center[1, gi, gj, 3]), Y=center[1, gi, gj, 1] / (-center[1, gi, gj, 3]), center=center, dt=6, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [1:1, 4:N + 3, N + 4:N + 6] = duo_extend_strip_n&lt;X=(-center[1, gi, gj, 1]) / center[1, gi, gj, 3], Y=(-center[1, gi, gj, 2]) / center[1, gi, gj, 3], center=center, dt=3, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [2:2, 1:3, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_w&lt;X=center[2, gi, gj, 2] / center[2, gi, gj, 1], Y=center[2, gi, gj, 3] / center[2, gi, gj, 1], center=center, dt=1, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [2:2, N + 4:N + 6, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_e&lt;X=(-center[2, gi, gj, 3]) / (-center[2, gi, gj, 1]), Y=(-center[2, gi, gj, 2]) / (-center[2, gi, gj, 1]), center=center, dt=4, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [2:2, 4:N + 3, 1:3] = duo_extend_strip_s&lt;X=center[2, gi, gj, 2] / (-center[2, gi, gj, 3]), Y=center[2, gi, gj, 1] / (-center[2, gi, gj, 3]), center=center, dt=6, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [2:2, 4:N + 3, N + 4:N + 6] = duo_extend_strip_n&lt;X=(-center[2, gi, gj, 1]) / center[2, gi, gj, 3], Y=(-center[2, gi, gj, 2]) / center[2, gi, gj, 3], center=center, dt=3, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [3:3, 1:3, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_w&lt;X=center[3, gi, gj, 2] / center[3, gi, gj, 1], Y=center[3, gi, gj, 3] / center[3, gi, gj, 1], center=center, dt=1, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [3:3, N + 4:N + 6, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_e&lt;X=(-center[3, gi, gj, 3]) / (-center[3, gi, gj, 1]), Y=(-center[3, gi, gj, 2]) / (-center[3, gi, gj, 1]), center=center, dt=4, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [3:3, 4:N + 3, 1:3] = duo_extend_strip_s&lt;X=(-center[3, gi, gj, 1]) / center[3, gi, gj, 2], Y=center[3, gi, gj, 3] / center[3, gi, gj, 2], center=center, dt=2, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [3:3, 4:N + 3, N + 4:N + 6] = duo_extend_strip_n&lt;X=(-center[3, gi, gj, 3]) / (-center[3, gi, gj, 2]), Y=center[3, gi, gj, 1] / (-center[3, gi, gj, 2]), center=center, dt=5, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [4:4, 1:3, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_w&lt;X=(-center[4, gi, gj, 1]) / center[4, gi, gj, 3], Y=(-center[4, gi, gj, 2]) / center[4, gi, gj, 3], center=center, dt=3, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [4:4, N + 4:N + 6, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_e&lt;X=center[4, gi, gj, 2] / (-center[4, gi, gj, 3]), Y=center[4, gi, gj, 1] / (-center[4, gi, gj, 3]), center=center, dt=6, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [4:4, 4:N + 3, 1:3] = duo_extend_strip_s&lt;X=(-center[4, gi, gj, 1]) / center[4, gi, gj, 2], Y=center[4, gi, gj, 3] / center[4, gi, gj, 2], center=center, dt=2, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [4:4, 4:N + 3, N + 4:N + 6] = duo_extend_strip_n&lt;X=(-center[4, gi, gj, 3]) / (-center[4, gi, gj, 2]), Y=center[4, gi, gj, 1] / (-center[4, gi, gj, 2]), center=center, dt=5, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [5:5, 1:3, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_w&lt;X=(-center[5, gi, gj, 1]) / center[5, gi, gj, 3], Y=(-center[5, gi, gj, 2]) / center[5, gi, gj, 3], center=center, dt=3, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [5:5, N + 4:N + 6, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_e&lt;X=center[5, gi, gj, 2] / (-center[5, gi, gj, 3]), Y=center[5, gi, gj, 1] / (-center[5, gi, gj, 3]), center=center, dt=6, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [5:5, 4:N + 3, 1:3] = duo_extend_strip_s&lt;X=(-center[5, gi, gj, 3]) / (-center[5, gi, gj, 1]), Y=(-center[5, gi, gj, 2]) / (-center[5, gi, gj, 1]), center=center, dt=4, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [5:5, 4:N + 3, N + 4:N + 6] = duo_extend_strip_n&lt;X=center[5, gi, gj, 2] / center[5, gi, gj, 1], Y=center[5, gi, gj, 3] / center[5, gi, gj, 1], center=center, dt=1, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [6:6, 1:3, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_w&lt;X=(-center[6, gi, gj, 3]) / (-center[6, gi, gj, 2]), Y=center[6, gi, gj, 1] / (-center[6, gi, gj, 2]), center=center, dt=5, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [6:6, N + 4:N + 6, 4:N + 3] = duo_extend_strip_e&lt;X=(-center[6, gi, gj, 1]) / center[6, gi, gj, 2], Y=center[6, gi, gj, 3] / center[6, gi, gj, 2], center=center, dt=2, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [6:6, 4:N + 3, 1:3] = duo_extend_strip_s&lt;X=(-center[6, gi, gj, 3]) / (-center[6, gi, gj, 1]), Y=(-center[6, gi, gj, 2]) / (-center[6, gi, gj, 1]), center=center, dt=4, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;, [6:6, 4:N + 3, N + 4:N + 6] = duo_extend_strip_n&lt;X=center[6, gi, gj, 2] / center[6, gi, gj, 1], Y=center[6, gi, gj, 3] / center[6, gi, gj, 1], center=center, dt=1, q=q, refx=refx, refy=refy&gt;)</div>

Halo extension of a cell field into the 3-layer donor-cube halo (Mouallem et al. duo-grid). The interior copy is region 0; each of the 24 tile-edge strips applies a shared per-edge remap template (duo_extend_strip_{w,e,s,n}): a tent-basis (bilinear) interpolation in the donor tile's gnomonic coordinates X, Y with one-sided linear extrapolation past the donor edge. X and Y are bound per strip as the signed face-rotation of the halo cell's Cartesian center into the donor frame; dt is the donor tile.

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES, 13, e2020MS002280. https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280

**Conformance fixtures**

- simulation: [`duo_swe_dp`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/simulation/duo_swe_dp) — status `active`
- simulation: [`duo_swe_freestream`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/simulation/duo_swe_freestream) — status `active`
- simulation: [`duo_swe_ua`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/simulation/duo_swe_ua) — status `active`
- simulation: [`duo_swe_va`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/simulation/duo_swe_va) — status `active`

### `duo_wind_cov_u`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:wind_rotate</code> <code>order:0</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x_cell_ext,y_cell_ext</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/duo_wind_cov_u.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/duo_wind_cov_u.esm)

**Definition:**

<div class="math-block">sum[gt, gi, gj] (duo_wind_assy_6&lt;center=center, p1=gi, p2=center[gt, ifelse(gi &lt; N + 6, gi + 1, gi - 1), gj, 1], p3=center[gt, ifelse(gi &lt; N + 6, gi + 1, gi - 1), gj, 3], p4=center[gt, ifelse(gi &lt; N + 6, gi + 1, gi - 1), gj, 2], uz=uz, vm=vm&gt; + duo_wind_assy_5&lt;center=center, p1=gi, p2=center[gt, ifelse(gi &lt; N + 6, gi + 1, gi - 1), gj, 2], p3=center[gt, ifelse(gi &lt; N + 6, gi + 1, gi - 1), gj, 1], p4=center[gt, ifelse(gi &lt; N + 6, gi + 1, gi - 1), gj, 3], uz=uz, vm=vm&gt; + duo_wind_assy_4&lt;center=center, p1=gi, p2=center[gt, ifelse(gi &lt; N + 6, gi + 1, gi - 1), gj, 3], p3=center[gt, ifelse(gi &lt; N + 6, gi + 1, gi - 1), gj, 2], p4=center[gt, ifelse(gi &lt; N + 6, gi + 1, gi - 1), gj, 1], uz=uz, vm=vm&gt;) where {gi in x_cell_ext, gj in y_cell_ext, gt in tile}</div>

Rotate EARTH-RELATIVE halo winds (uz,vm) back to A-grid COVARIANT (ua tile-x covariant) at each halo cell -- step 4 of the Mouallem 2023 vector halo remap, after scalar duo_extend fills the earth-relative components across seams. V=uz*elon+vm*elat then dotted with the node-tangent basis (from duo_cell_center); the tangent x/y neighbour is the +1 cell, or -1 with a sign flip at the outermost halo cell gi/gj=N+6. Params [uz,vm,center]; bind center=duo_cell_center; R_sphere free name.

**References**

- Mouallem, J., Harris, L., & Chen, X. (2023). Implementation of the novel Duo-Grid in GFDL's FV3. JAMES, 15, e2023MS003712.

**Conformance fixtures**

- ast: [`duo_wind_cov_u`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/ast/duo_wind_cov_u) — status `active`

### `duo_wind_cov_v`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:wind_rotate</code> <code>order:0</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x_cell_ext,y_cell_ext</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/duo_wind_cov_v.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/duo_wind_cov_v.esm)

**Definition:**

<div class="math-block">sum[gt, gi, gj] (duo_wind_assy_6&lt;center=center, p1=gj, p2=center[gt, gi, ifelse(gj &lt; N + 6, gj + 1, gj - 1), 1], p3=center[gt, gi, ifelse(gj &lt; N + 6, gj + 1, gj - 1), 3], p4=center[gt, gi, ifelse(gj &lt; N + 6, gj + 1, gj - 1), 2], uz=uz, vm=vm&gt; + duo_wind_assy_5&lt;center=center, p1=gj, p2=center[gt, gi, ifelse(gj &lt; N + 6, gj + 1, gj - 1), 2], p3=center[gt, gi, ifelse(gj &lt; N + 6, gj + 1, gj - 1), 1], p4=center[gt, gi, ifelse(gj &lt; N + 6, gj + 1, gj - 1), 3], uz=uz, vm=vm&gt; + duo_wind_assy_4&lt;center=center, p1=gj, p2=center[gt, gi, ifelse(gj &lt; N + 6, gj + 1, gj - 1), 3], p3=center[gt, gi, ifelse(gj &lt; N + 6, gj + 1, gj - 1), 2], p4=center[gt, gi, ifelse(gj &lt; N + 6, gj + 1, gj - 1), 1], uz=uz, vm=vm&gt;) where {gi in x_cell_ext, gj in y_cell_ext, gt in tile}</div>

Rotate EARTH-RELATIVE halo winds (uz,vm) back to A-grid COVARIANT (va tile-y covariant) at each halo cell -- step 4 of the Mouallem 2023 vector halo remap, after scalar duo_extend fills the earth-relative components across seams. V=uz*elon+vm*elat then dotted with the node-tangent basis (from duo_cell_center); the tangent x/y neighbour is the +1 cell, or -1 with a sign flip at the outermost halo cell gi/gj=N+6. Params [uz,vm,center]; bind center=duo_cell_center; R_sphere free name.

**References**

- Mouallem, J., Harris, L., & Chen, X. (2023). Implementation of the novel Duo-Grid in GFDL's FV3. JAMES, 15, e2023MS003712.

**Conformance fixtures**

### `duo_wind_earth_u`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:wind_rotate</code> <code>order:0</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x,y</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/duo_wind_earth_u.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/duo_wind_earth_u.esm)

**Definition:**

<div class="math-block">sum[gt, gi, gj] ((duo_wind_assy_14&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], p2=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], p4=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], p5=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], ua=ua, va=va&gt; + duo_wind_assy_13&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], p2=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], p4=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], p5=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], ua=ua, va=va&gt;) * (-sin(atan2(center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1]))) + (duo_wind_assy_14&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], p2=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], p4=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], p5=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], ua=ua, va=va&gt; + duo_wind_assy_13&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], p2=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], p4=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], p5=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], ua=ua, va=va&gt;) * cos(atan2(center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1])) + (duo_wind_assy_14&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], p2=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], p4=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], p5=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], ua=ua, va=va&gt; + duo_wind_assy_13&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], p2=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], p4=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], p5=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], ua=ua, va=va&gt;) * 0) where {gi in x, gj in y, gt in tile}</div>

Rotate A-grid COVARIANT winds (ua,va) to EARTH-RELATIVE (zonal component) at each cell centre -- step 2 of the Mouallem 2023 vector halo remap. Physical velocity from the covariant components via the metric raise g^ij=(1/sin2A)[[1,-cosA],[-cosA,1]] with the node-tangent basis e1,e2 (e_i=normalize(cross(cross(ka,kother),ka)) from duo_cell_center), then dotted with the local zonal (elon) unit vector (elon=(-sinL,cosL,0) / elat=(-sinP cosL,-sinP sinL,cosP), lon=atan2, lat=asin from the cell centre). Earth-relative winds are globally continuous, so the halo is then filled by the scalar duo_extend and rotated back by duo_wind_cov_u. Params [ua,va,center]; bind center=duo_cell_center; R_sphere free name. Verified to ~3e-16 vs a direct reimplementation.

**References**

- Mouallem, J., Harris, L., & Chen, X. (2023). Implementation of the novel Duo-Grid in GFDL's FV3. JAMES, 15, e2023MS003712.

**Conformance fixtures**

- ast: [`duo_wind_earth_u`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/ast/duo_wind_earth_u) — status `active`

### `duo_wind_earth_v`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:wind_rotate</code> <code>order:0</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x,y</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/duo_wind_earth_v.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/duo_wind_earth_v.esm)

**Definition:**

<div class="math-block">sum[gt, gi, gj] ((duo_wind_assy_14&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], p2=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], p4=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], p5=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], ua=ua, va=va&gt; + duo_wind_assy_13&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], p2=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], p4=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], p5=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], ua=ua, va=va&gt;) * (-sin(asin(center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] / R_sphere)) * cos(atan2(center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1]))) + (duo_wind_assy_14&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], p2=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], p4=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], p5=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], ua=ua, va=va&gt; + duo_wind_assy_13&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], p2=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], p4=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], p5=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], ua=ua, va=va&gt;) * (-sin(asin(center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] / R_sphere)) * sin(atan2(center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1]))) + (duo_wind_assy_14&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], p2=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 3], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], p4=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 2], p5=center[gt, gi + 4, gj + 3, 1], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], ua=ua, va=va&gt; + duo_wind_assy_13&lt;center=center, p1=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 2], p2=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 3], p3=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3], p4=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 2], p5=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 4, 1], p6=center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 1], ua=ua, va=va&gt;) * cos(asin(center[gt, gi + 3, gj + 3, 3] / R_sphere))) where {gi in x, gj in y, gt in tile}</div>

Rotate A-grid COVARIANT winds (ua,va) to EARTH-RELATIVE (meridional component) at each cell centre -- step 2 of the Mouallem 2023 vector halo remap. Physical velocity from the covariant components via the metric raise g^ij=(1/sin2A)[[1,-cosA],[-cosA,1]] with the node-tangent basis e1,e2 (e_i=normalize(cross(cross(ka,kother),ka)) from duo_cell_center), then dotted with the local meridional (elat) unit vector (elon=(-sinL,cosL,0) / elat=(-sinP cosL,-sinP sinL,cosP), lon=atan2, lat=asin from the cell centre). Earth-relative winds are globally continuous, so the halo is then filled by the scalar duo_extend and rotated back by duo_wind_cov_v. Params [ua,va,center]; bind center=duo_cell_center; R_sphere free name. Verified to ~3e-16 vs a direct reimplementation.

**References**

- Mouallem, J., Harris, L., & Chen, X. (2023). Implementation of the novel Duo-Grid in GFDL's FV3. JAMES, 15, e2023MS003712.

### `flux_divergence_duo_g1`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:flux_divergence</code> <code>order:1</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x,y</code> <code>stencil width 1</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/flux_divergence_duo_g1.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/flux_divergence_duo_g1.esm)

**Discretization** — one `makearray` tiling the output axes; boundary conditions live in the face regions of this same rule (esm-spec §9.6.8; later regions overwrite earlier, §4.3.2). Imported stencils appear as template applications `name<param=value, ...>`, defined in the Stencils section of this page:

<div class="math-block">makearray([1:6, 1:N, 1:N] = flux_divergence_duo_g1_interior&lt;area=area, center=center, cosA=cosA, dp=dp, node=node, ua=ua, va=va&gt;)</div>

Continuity (mass) tendency dh/dt for the duo gnomonic cubed-sphere shallow-water core, Ng=1 (Nc=1-point volume-mean reconstruction, design order 1). Discretises Chen (2021) Eqs 3-6 exactly as the Zenodo reference sw_full: dp/dt = (fx(i)-fx(i+1)+fy(j)-fy(j+1))/A with the LMARS face mass flux fx = u* * Lx * dp_upwind. u* is the LMARS normal velocity Eq 11 with the wave speed a=1/2(sqrt(dp_L)+sqrt(dp_R)) from CELL depth (the paper's build default, no inline_cs); the interface states come from raise-then-reconstruct: the contravariant u^1=(ua-cosA va)/sin^2 a (and v^2 on y-faces) is volume-mean reconstructed to the face and multiplied by the face sin(alpha), and dp is reconstructed and upwinded on sign(u*) (Eqs 14-15). Lx/Ly are the great-circle face lengths (duo_arc_length on the corner nodes); A is duo_cell_area; cosA at cells is duo_cos_alpha (node-sourced, spanning the N+6 halo the Ng=3 stencil reaches); face sin(alpha) is duo_tangent_cos at the face. Operates on the halo-EXTENDED prognostic fields (duo_extend output), so a single interior region [tile,x,y] tiles the whole tendency with no boundary stencil. Applied by name from the duo SWE problem. Method-of-lines: this is the pure spatial RHS (no rk2/rk3, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. Journal of Advances in Modeling Earth Systems, 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

**Conformance fixtures**

- ast: [`duo_flux_divergence_g1`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/ast/duo_flux_divergence_g1) — status `active`

### `momentum_u_duo_g1`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:momentum_u</code> <code>order:1</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x,y</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/momentum_u_duo_g1.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/momentum_u_duo_g1.esm)

**Discretization** — one `makearray` tiling the output axes; boundary conditions live in the face regions of this same rule (esm-spec §9.6.8; later regions overwrite earlier, §4.3.2). Imported stencils appear as template applications `name<param=value, ...>`, defined in the Stencils section of this page:

<div class="math-block">makearray([1:6, 1:N, 1:N] = momentum_u_duo_g1_interior&lt;area=area, center=center, cosA=cosA, dp=dp, f=f, node=node, ua=ua, va=va&gt;)</div>

Vector-invariant momentum tendency for the covariant u-wind, duo gnomonic cubed-sphere SWE core, Ng=1, WITHOUT the pressure gradient (added by a separate pgrad_*_u rule so both L97 and direct forms are drop-in). Chen (2021) C36/C37 as the Zenodo sw_full: du/dt = +(v^contra)*sin(alpha)*(f+zeta) + (ke(i)-ke(i+1))/Dx. The absolute vorticity f+zeta is the circulation [dx(vp*Lx)-dy(up*Ly)]/A + f with up,vp the UPWINDED parallel velocities reused from the LMARS solve (Eq 9/C38); the interface kinetic energy is 1/2(u*^2 + up^2) in the local orthogonal frame (Eq 10); (f+zeta) multiplies the contravariant cross-velocity times sin(alpha)=sqrt(g) (C36/C37); Dx is the great-circle arc between the two x-face midpoints. Operates on halo-extended dp,ua,va; f is the problem-supplied Coriolis parameter 2 Omega sin(theta) on [tile,x,y]. Method-of-lines spatial RHS.

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

**Conformance fixtures**

- ast: [`duo_momentum_u_duo_g1`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/ast/duo_momentum_u_duo_g1) — status `active`

### `momentum_v_duo_g1`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:momentum_v</code> <code>order:1</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x,y</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/momentum_v_duo_g1.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/momentum_v_duo_g1.esm)

Imported stencils appear as template applications `name<param=value, ...>`, defined in the Stencils section of this page:

<div class="math-block">makearray([1:6, 1:N, 1:N] = momentum_v_duo_g1_interior&lt;area=area, center=center, cosA=cosA, dp=dp, f=f, node=node, ua=ua, va=va&gt;)</div>

Vector-invariant momentum tendency for the covariant v-wind, duo gnomonic cubed-sphere SWE core, Ng=1, WITHOUT the pressure gradient (added by a separate pgrad_*_v rule so both L97 and direct forms are drop-in). Chen (2021) C36/C37 as the Zenodo sw_full: dv/dt = -(u^contra)*sin(alpha)*(f+zeta) + (ke(i)-ke(i+1))/Dy. The absolute vorticity f+zeta is the circulation [dx(vp*Lx)-dy(up*Ly)]/A + f with up,vp the UPWINDED parallel velocities reused from the LMARS solve (Eq 9/C38); the interface kinetic energy is 1/2(u*^2 + up^2) in the local orthogonal frame (Eq 10); (f+zeta) multiplies the contravariant cross-velocity times sin(alpha)=sqrt(g) (C36/C37); Dy is the great-circle arc between the two y-face midpoints. Operates on halo-extended dp,ua,va; f is the problem-supplied Coriolis parameter 2 Omega sin(theta) on [tile,x,y]. Method-of-lines spatial RHS.

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

**Conformance fixtures**

### `pgrad_direct_u_duo_g1`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:pgrad_direct_u</code> <code>order:1</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x,y</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/pgrad_direct_u_duo_g1.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/pgrad_direct_u_duo_g1.esm)

**Discretization** — one `makearray` tiling the output axes; boundary conditions live in the face regions of this same rule (esm-spec §9.6.8; later regions overwrite earlier, §4.3.2). Imported stencils appear as template applications `name<param=value, ...>`, defined in the Stencils section of this page:

<div class="math-block">makearray([1:6, 1:N, 1:N] = pgrad_direct_u_duo_g1_interior&lt;center=center, cosA=cosA, dp=dp, node=node, phis=phis, ua=ua, va=va&gt;)</div>

Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant u-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=1. Direct LMARS-Pi* pressure gradient -dx(Pibar + Pivis + Phi_s)/Dx, mathematically equivalent to the L97 four-corner form for SW (paper) but written as a plain interface-pressure difference. Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dx the arc between x-face midpoints. Add to momentum_u_duo_g1 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

### `pgrad_l97_u_duo_g1`

<p class="rule-meta"><code>op:pgrad_l97_u</code> <code>order:1</code> <code>bc:duo</code> <code>axes:tile,x,y</code></p>

Source: [`grids/duo/rules/pgrad_l97_u_duo_g1.esm`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/blob/main/grids/duo/rules/pgrad_l97_u_duo_g1.esm)

**Discretization** — one `makearray` tiling the output axes; boundary conditions live in the face regions of this same rule (esm-spec §9.6.8; later regions overwrite earlier, §4.3.2). Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant u-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=1. L97 four-corner geometric pressure-gradient (Lin 1997) plus the LMARS viscous term, the Zenodo sw_full DEFAULT (Eqs 20-26; akap=1, ptop=0 for SW so pk=pe, gz=Phi_s+Pi and the four-corner du_hydro reduces to the geometric -dx(avg(Phi_s+Pi))/Dx, with the viscous du_pp = rdx/dp*(Pibar_i*Pivis_i - Pibar_i+1*Pivis_i+1)). Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dx the arc between x-face midpoints. Add to momentum_u_duo_g1 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

Pressure-gradient tendency for the covariant u-wind, duo SWE core, Ng=3. L97 four-corner geometric pressure-gradient (Lin 1997) plus the LMARS viscous term, the Zenodo sw_full DEFAULT (Eqs 20-26; akap=1, ptop=0 for SW so pk=pe, gz=Phi_s+Pi and the four-corner du_hydro reduces to the geometric -dx(avg(Phi_s+Pi))/Dx, with the viscous du_pp = rdx/dp*(Pibar_i*Pivis_i - Pibar_i+1*Pivis_i+1)). Pibar = 1/2(dpe+dpw) is the symmetric M**vm face average of the depth pressure (= 1/2 of the two reconstructed interface pressures); Sbar the same for the surface geopotential Phi_s (problem-supplied, 0 for no topography); Pivis = a/2(uE-uW) the LMARS viscous pressure; Dx the arc between x-face midpoints. Add to momentum_u_duo_g3 for the full tendency. Method-of-lines spatial RHS (beta=1, no forward-backward).

**References**

- Chen, X. (2021). The LMARS based shallow-water dynamical core on generic gnomonic cubed-sphere geometry. JAMES 13(1), e2020MS002280. [`doi:10.1029/2020MS002280`](https://doi.org/10.1029/2020MS002280)

- simulation: [`duo_swe_freestream`](https://github.com/EarthSciML/earthscidiscretizations/tree/main/tests/conformance/simulation/duo_swe_freestream) — status `active`
